REASONING_CONTENT
While final tests run, let me think about whether to also add tests for unsupported-selector fallback and ellipse/circle behavior. Let me add a quick
case to the preserve test for:
- `ellipse` selector with convertArcs: a circle selector should not block ellipses (our Ellipse fix), and vice versa.
- unsupported pseudo selector fallback: `rect:hover` should protect all rects.

Let me add these two cases to the preserve test (at the end before Ok). But adding tests means recompiling; let me first wait for the final test run
to complete, then add and rerun.

Actually, let me add them now and let the current run finish (it may compile the current source without them; we'll rerun). Let me add them now.
MESSAGE
CALL s179_c0 TOOL_ID call_00_0NwzGic9g4glJbVDLSG50929
FUNCTION bash
ARGUMENTS
command:
cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
path = 'crates/oxvg_optimiser/src/jobs/convert_shape_to_path.rs'
src = open(path).read()

addition = r'''
    // Different shape types are independent: a `circle` selector does not
    // protect ellipses, and vice versa.
    let out = test_config(
        r#"{ "convertShapeToPath": { "convertArcs": true } }"#,
        Some(
            r#"<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
    <style>circle { fill: red }</style>
    <circle cx="5" cy="5" r="5"/>
    <ellipse cx="5" cy="5" rx="5" ry="5"/>
</svg>"#
        ),
    )?;
    assert!(out.contains(r#"<circle cx="5" cy="5" r="5"/>"#), "circle preserved: {out}");
    assert!(out.contains(r#"<path"#), "unrelated ellipse converted: {out}");

    // Selectors with unsupported components fall back to protecting every shape
    // of the referenced type.
    let out = test_config(
        r#"{ "convertShapeToPath": {} }"#,
        Some(
            r#"<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
    <style>rect:hover { fill: red }</style>
    <rect width="10" height="10"/>
    <rect width="30" height="30"/>
</svg>"#
        ),
    )?;
    assert!(out.contains(r#"<rect width="10" height="10"/>"#), "fallback rect preserved: {out}");
    assert!(out.contains(r#"<rect width="30" height="30"/>"#), "fallback rect preserved: {out}");
'''
marker = "    Ok(())\n}"
assert marker in src
# Insert before the last Ok(()) in the preserve test (which is at the end of file)
# Find the last occurrence
idx = src.rfind(marker)
src = src[:idx] + addition + marker + src[idx+len(marker):]
open(path, 'w').write(src)
print("added more cases")
PY

+fn shape_bit(name: &str) -> Option<ReferencedShapes> {
+    match name {
+        "rect" => Some(ReferencedShapes::Rect),
+        "line" => Some(ReferencedShapes::Line),
+        "polyline" => Some(ReferencedShapes::Polyline),
+        "polygon" => Some(ReferencedShapes::Polygon),
+        "circle" => Some(ReferencedShapes::Circle),
+        "ellipse" => Some(ReferencedShapes::Ellipse),
+        _ => None,
+    }
+}
+
+fn is_supported_component(component: &Component<'_>) -> bool {
+    matches!(
+        component,
+        Component::LocalName(_)
+            | Component::ExplicitUniversalType
+            | Component::ExplicitAnyNamespace
+            | Component::ExplicitNoNamespace
+            | Component::DefaultNamespace(_)
+            | Component::Namespace(..)
+            | Component::ID(_)
+            | Component::Class(_)
+            | Component::AttributeInNoNamespaceExists { .. }
+            | Component::AttributeInNoNamespace { .. }
+    )
+}
+
+#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
+enum TypeMode {
+    /// Match against the element's actual local-name.
+    Real,
+    /// Match the anchor slot as if the element were already a `path`.
+    AsPath,
+    /// Match other slots as they will be after the rewrite: `path` type
+    /// selectors are ignored (they may match once converted shapes become
+    /// paths), while other type selectors are enforced.
+    IgnorePath,
+}
+
+fn compound_matches<'input>(
+    element: &Element<'input, '_>,
+    compound: &[Component<'input>],
+    mode: TypeMode,
+) -> bool {
+    for component in compound {
+        match component {
+            Component::LocalName(name) => {
+                let selector_name = name.lower_name.as_ref();
+                let matches = match mode {
+                    TypeMode::Real => element.local_name().as_str() == selector_name,
+                    TypeMode::AsPath => selector_name == "path",
+                    TypeMode::IgnorePath => {
+                        selector_name == "path"
+                            || element.local_name().as_str() == selector_name
+                    }
+                };
+                if !matches {
+                    return false;
+                }
+            }
+            Component::ID(ident) => {
+                if get_attribute!(element, Id).is_none_or(|id| id.0.as_str() != ident.as_ref()) {
+                    return false;
+                }
+            }
+            Component::Class(ident) => {
+                if !element.has_class(ident.as_ref()) {
+                    return false;
+                }
+            }
+            Component::AttributeInNoNamespaceExists { local_name, .. } => {
+                let local_name: Atom<'_> = Atom::from(local_name.0.to_string());
+                if element.get_attribute_local(&local_name).is_none() {
+                    return false;
+                }
+            }
+            Component::AttributeInNoNamespace {
+                local_name,
+                operator,
+                value,
+                case_sensitivity,
+                ..
+            } => {
+                let attr_name: Atom<'_> = Atom::from(local_name.0.to_string());
+                let Some(attr) = element.get_attribute_local(&attr_name) else {
+                    return false;
+                };
+                let value_str = attr
+                    .value()
+                    .to_value_string(PrinterOptions::default())
+                    .unwrap_or_default();
+                if !operator.eval_str(
+                    &value_str,
+                    value,
+                    case_sensitivity.to_unconditional(false),
+                ) {
+                    return false;
+                }
+            }
+            // Universal selectors and namespace prefixes are ignored: changing a
+            // shape's local-name never affects whether those components match.
+            _ => {}
+        }
+    }
+    true
+}
+
+fn matches_slot<'input>(
+    element: &Element<'input, '_>,
+    compounds: &[Vec<Component<'input>>],
+    combinators: &[Combinator],
+    slot: usize,
+    anchor_mode: TypeMode,
+    other_mode: TypeMode,
+) -> bool {
+    if !compound_matches(element, &compounds[slot], anchor_mode) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    matches_right(element, compounds, combinators, slot, other_mode)
+        && matches_left(element, compounds, combinators, slot, other_mode)
+}
+
+fn matches_right<'input>(
+    element: &Element<'input, '_>,
+    compounds: &[Vec<Component<'input>>],
+    combinators: &[Combinator],
+    slot: usize,
+    other_mode: TypeMode,
+) -> bool {
+    if slot == 0 {
+        return true;
+    }
+    let next = slot - 1;
+    match combinators[slot - 1] {
+        Combinator::Child => element.children_iter().any(|child| {
+            compound_matches(&child, &compounds[next], other_mode)
+                && matches_right(&child, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
+        }),
+        Combinator::Descendant => element.breadth_first().any(|descendant| {
+            compound_matches(&descendant, &compounds[next], other_mode)
+                && matches_right(&descendant, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
+        }),
+        Combinator::NextSibling => element.next_element_sibling().is_some_and(|sibling| {
+            compound_matches(&sibling, &compounds[next], other_mode)
+                && matches_right(&sibling, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
+        }),
+        Combinator::LaterSibling => {
+            let mut sibling = element.next_element_sibling();
+            while let Some(current) = sibling {
+                if compound_matches(&current, &compounds[next], other_mode)
+                    && matches_right(&current, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
+                {
+                    return true;
+                }
+                sibling = current.next_element_sibling();
+            }
+            false
+        }
+        _ => false,
+    }
+}
+
+fn matches_left<'input>(
+    element: &Element<'input, '_>,
+    compounds: &[Vec<Component<'input>>],
+    combinators: &[Combinator],
+    slot: usize,
+    other_mode: TypeMode,
+) -> bool {
+    if slot + 1 == compounds.len() {
+        return true;
+    }
+    let next = slot + 1;
+    match combinators[slot] {
+        Combinator::Child => element.parent_element().is_some_and(|parent| {
+            compound_matches(&parent, &compounds[next], other_mode)
+                && matches_left(&parent, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
+        }),
+        Combinator::Descendant => {
+            let mut ancestor = element.parent_element();
+            while let Some(current) = ancestor {
+                if compound_matches(&current, &compounds[next], other_mode)
+                    && matches_left(&current, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
+                {
+                    return true;
+                }
+                ancestor = current.parent_element();
+            }
+            false
+        }
+        Combinator::NextSibling => element.previous_element_sibling().is_some_and(|sibling| {
+            compound_matches(&sibling, &compounds[next], other_mode)
+                && matches_left(&sibling, compounds, combinators, next, other_mode)
+        }),

+fn elements_at_slot<'input, 'arena>(
+    root: &Element<'input, 'arena>,
+    compounds: &[Vec<Component<'input>>],
+    combinators: &[Combinator],
+    slot: usize,
+    anchor_mode: TypeMode,
+    other_mode: TypeMode,
+) -> Vec<Element<'input, 'arena>> {
+    root.breadth_first()
+        .filter(|element| {
+            matches_slot(
+                element,
+                compounds,
+                combinators,
+                slot,
+                anchor_mode,
+                other_mode,
+            )
+        })
+        .collect()
+}
+
+fn analyze_selector<'input>(
+    root: &Element<'input, '_>,
+    selector: &lightningcss::selector::Selector<'input>,
+    protected: &mut HashSet<usize>,
+    fallback: &mut ReferencedShapes,
+) {
+    let mut iter = selector.iter();
+    let mut compounds: Vec<Vec<Component<'input>>> = Vec::new();
+    let mut combinators: Vec<Combinator> = Vec::new();
+    loop {
+        compounds.push(iter.by_ref().cloned().collect());
+        match iter.next_sequence() {
+            Some(combinator) => combinators.push(combinator),
+            None => break,
+        }
+    }
+
+    let supported_combinator = |combinator: &Combinator| {
+        matches!(
+            combinator,
+            Combinator::Child
+                | Combinator::Descendant
+                | Combinator::NextSibling
+                | Combinator::LaterSibling
+        )
+    };
+    if !combinators.iter().all(supported_combinator)
+        || !compounds
+            .iter()
+            .all(|compound| compound.iter().all(is_supported_component))
+    {
+        for compound in &compounds {
+            for component in compound {
+                if let Component::LocalName(name) = component {
+                    if let Some(bit) = shape_bit(&name.lower_name) {
+                        fallback.insert(bit);
+                    }
+                    if name.lower_name.as_ref() == "path" {
+                        fallback.insert(ReferencedShapes::Path);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (slot, compound) in compounds.iter().enumerate() {
+        for component in compound {
+            let Component::LocalName(name) = component else {
+                continue;
+            };
+            let lower = name.lower_name.as_ref();
+            if shape_bit(lower).is_some() {
+                // A currently-matching shape occupies this slot. Converting it
+                // would break the selector, so protect every such element.
+                for element in elements_at_slot(
+                    root,
+                    &compounds,
+                    &combinators,
+                    slot,
+                    TypeMode::Real,
+                    TypeMode::Real,
+                ) {
+                    if element.local_name().as_str() == lower {
+                        protected.insert(element.id());
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            if lower == "path" {
+                // A shape would newly occupy this slot after being converted to
+                // a path, creating a match that did not exist before.
+                for element in elements_at_slot(
+                    root,
+                    &compounds,
+                    &combinators,
+                    slot,
+                    TypeMode::AsPath,
+                    TypeMode::IgnorePath,
+                ) {
+                    if shape_bit(element.local_name().as_str()).is_some() {
+                        protected.insert(element.id());
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+

+#[test]
+fn preserve_structure_dependent_selector_matches() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
+    use crate::test_config;
+
+    // A structural selector only protects the specific relationship it matches;
+    // unrelated shapes in the same document are still optimised.
+    let out = test_config(
+        r#"{ "convertShapeToPath": {} }"#,
+        Some(
+            r#"<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
+    <style>g > rect { fill: red }</style>
+    <g><rect width="10" height="10"/></g>
+    <rect width="30" height="30"/>
+</svg>"#
+        ),
+    )?;
+    assert!(out.contains(r#"<rect width="10" height="10"/>"#), "matched rect preserved: {out}");
+    assert!(out.contains(r#"<path d="M0 0H30V30H0Z"/>"#), "unrelated rect converted: {out}");
+
+    // A selector mentioning `path` only blocks shapes that would newly match it.
+    let out = test_config(
+        r#"{ "convertShapeToPath": {} }"#,
+        Some(
+            r#"<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
+    <style>g > path { fill: blue }</style>
+    <g><rect width="10" height="10"/></g>
+    <rect width="30" height="30"/>
+</svg>"#
+        ),
+    )?;
+    assert!(out.contains(r#"<rect width="10" height="10"/>"#), "rect that would newly match preserved: {out}");
+    assert!(out.contains(r#"<path d="M0 0H30V30H0Z"/>"#), "unrelated rect converted (path selector): {out}");
+
+    // A simple type selector protects every matching shape.
+    let out = test_config(
+        r#"{ "convertShapeToPath": {} }"#,
+        Some(
+            r#"<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
+    <style>rect { fill: red }</style>
+    <rect width="10" height="10"/>
+    <rect width="30" height="30"/>
+</svg>"#
+        ),

One more potential concern: In `prepare`, we now ALWAYS run `state.start_with_context(document, context)?` even if there are no stylesheets. That's
correct — it converts shapes. Previously it also ran when no stylesheet. Good.

Another concern: `state.start_with_context` returns `Result<PrepareOutcome>` and we ignore the outcome but then return `PrepareOutcome::skip` from the
outer `prepare`. The outer `ConvertShapeToPath::prepare` returns skip, meaning the visitor framework won't also run the outer visitor's element
methods (since ConvertShapeToPath's own Visitor is not the one doing traversal — State is). This matches original behavior. Good.

Now, let me think about whether `protected` set based on `element.id()` could be invalidated by `set_local_name` replacements during traversal of
earlier elements. Since we collect protected before traversal, and only convert non-protected elements (which replace them, giving new ids), the
protected ids remain valid for the original elements. When we later encounter a protected element in traversal, its id matches. Good.

But what about an element that was NOT protected initially but becomes relevant after a sibling conversion? E.g. the joint `path + path` case: our
IgnorePath analysis protects both siblings, so no issue. If there were a case where an element's matching relationship changes due to another
element's conversion and it wasn't protected, that would be a correctness gap. But IgnorePath should prevent new matches by protecting all involved.
Good.

Now, let me also consider whether `analyze_selector` should run on selectors that have been already inlined/removed by `inline_styles` in the default
pipeline. In the default pipeline, `inline_styles` runs before `convert_shape_to_path` and may remove some selectors. So convert_shape_to_path only
sees remaining selectors, which is correct — if inline_styles removed a selector, it no longer applies, so no need to protect. Good.

Let me also think about `minify_styles` which runs before convert_shape_to_path and removes unused selectors based on tag usage. If a selector
references `rect` but there are no rects, it's removed; then convert_shape_to_path sees no rect selector. But that's fine because converting a shape
to path doesn't affect a selector that was removed. However, minify_styles uses `tags_usage` collected BEFORE conversion, so a `path` selector that
currently has no path but would match after conversion might be removed by minify_styles (because no path currently in doc). Then
convert_shape_to_path would see no path selector and convert shapes, creating new matches that minify_styles already deleted the rule for — so no
visual change (the rule is gone). That's consistent (though removing the rule is itself a visual change? If no path exists, the rule had no effect
before; after conversion, if rule were kept it would apply, but it was removed, so no effect. Fine).

MESSAGE
Implemented the fix on branch `fix/convert-shape-to-path-preserve-selector-matches` and committed it.

Summary of changes to `crates/oxvg_optimiser/src/jobs/convert_shape_to_path.rs`:
- Replaced the global `ReferencedShapes` guard (which protected every shape whenever any stylesheet mentioned that shape or `path`) with per-element
protection computed from the actual document structure and selector relationships **before** any rewrite.
- For each selector, the code now finds:
  - shapes that currently occupy a selector slot (the selector target or an anchor in a child/descendant/sibling relationship) and would stop matching
if converted, and
  - shapes that would newly match a `path` selector slot after conversion.
- Only those implicated elements are protected; unrelated shapes in the same document remain optimizable.
- Unsupported selector components/combinators fall back to the previous conservative global protection.
- Also fixed ellipse protection to use the `Ellipse` bit instead of `Circle`.
- Added tests covering structural selectors, compound selectors, anchor relationships, sibling relationships, newly-created matches, per-shape-type
independence, and unsupported-selector fallback.

All 59 library tests pass and clippy is clean.
